2026-10-25T08:17:53.8193172Z class LanguageInstallationTask(Task):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8193728Z     """Installation task for the language configuration."""
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8194190Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8195887Z     def __init__(self, sysroot, lang):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8196368Z         """Create a new task,
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8196711Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8197060Z         :param sysroot: a path to the root of the installed system
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8197683Z         :param lang: a value for LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8198189Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8198567Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8198995Z         self._sysroot = sysroot
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8199434Z         self._lang = lang
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8199759Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8200006Z     @property
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8200386Z     def name(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8200802Z         return "Configure language"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8201166Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8201413Z     def run(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8201834Z         """Run the installation task."""
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8202338Z         lang = self._get_supported_language()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8202870Z         self._write_language_configuration(lang)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8203511Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8203781Z     def _get_supported_language(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8204287Z         """Return a supported locale.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8204792Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8205276Z         :return: a locale
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8205737Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8206204Z         if self._is_language_support_installed(self._lang):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8206773Z             return self._lang
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8207114Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8207473Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale is unsupported.", self._lang)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8208177Z         log.debug("Using the '%s' locale as a fallback.", self.LOCALE_FALLBACK)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8208809Z         return self.LOCALE_FALLBACK
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8209196Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8209499Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8210121Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8210734Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8211094Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8211786Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8212267Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8212590Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8213286Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8213927Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8214296Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8214800Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8215521Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8216062Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8216615Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8216948Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8217300Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8217792Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8218122Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8218697Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8219052Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8303631Z             fobj.write('FONT="%s"\n' % vc_font)

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8319578Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8319765Z     def _from_localed_format(layouts, variants):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8320114Z         layouts = layouts.split(",") if layouts else []

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8320561Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8326110Z         :param convert: whether the layouts should be converted to a VConsole keymap

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8326980Z         if not self._localed_proxy:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327172Z             return
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327264Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327333Z         layouts = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327499Z         variants = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327671Z         parsing_failed = False
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327794Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327961Z         for layout_variant in (nonempty for nonempty in layouts_variants if nonempty):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8328258Z             try:

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8329793Z             layouts.append(layout)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8330003Z             variants.append(variant)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8330132Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8330219Z         if not layouts and parsing_failed:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8330422Z             return

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8354873Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355247Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355448Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355760Z     """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355844Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355913Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356090Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356205Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356283Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356498Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356726Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356890Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357160Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357334Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357489Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357880Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8362364Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8362538Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8363026Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8363434Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8363815Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8364180Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8364606Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8364952Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8365420Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8365849Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366112Z             #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366301Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366618Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366949Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8367273Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8367649Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8367907Z             #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368183Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368440Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368570Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368919Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369254Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369547Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369728Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369830Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370077Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370304Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370534Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370804Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371063Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371291Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371696Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371992Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8372326Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8372618Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8372879Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373176Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373492Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373707Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373810Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373970Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8400108Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8400369Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8400998Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8401345Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8401607Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8402100Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8402779Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8403146Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8403407Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8403747Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404209Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404457Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404549Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404869Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8405433Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8405771Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8406048Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8406489Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8406986Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8407325Z             return False

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8407605Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8407918Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8408452Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8408857Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8409133Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8409652Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8409851Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410004Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410377Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410662Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410949Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411121Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411314Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411626Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411869Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8412328Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8412622Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8413205Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8413884Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414057Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414175Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414558Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414853Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8416214Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8416968Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8417756Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8418420Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8418719Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8418950Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8419607Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8420003Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8420421Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8420690Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8420969Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8421334Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8421501Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8421644Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8422181Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8422742Z             return False
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8422912Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8423024Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8423386Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8423780Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424102Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424482Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424765Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424865Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8425548Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8426054Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8426489Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8426843Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8427114Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8427587Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8428084Z             return False

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528228Z from pyanaconda.core.constants import DEFAULT_KEYBOARD

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528437Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528516Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528523Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528690Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528793Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528796Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528870Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528984Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529057Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529185Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529258Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529345Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529485Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529629Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529714Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529822Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529969Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530054Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530160Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530235Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530351Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530424Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530528Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530605Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530671Z     """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530745Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530939Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531093Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531097Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531200Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531383Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531460Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531599Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531699Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531708Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531780Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531970Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532043Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532047Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532126Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532331Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532334Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532402Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532408Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532410Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532565Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532633Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532749Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532923Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532994Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533063Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533173Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533335Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533409Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533412Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533482Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8618539Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8618784Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619045Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619302Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619541Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619636Z #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619728Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619990Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620239Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620432Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620678Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620770Z #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620863Z set -eu
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620868Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620974Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621066Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621180Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621265Z do
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621365Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621460Z         in
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621567Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621683Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621847Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621944Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8622109Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8622192Z done
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8628140Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8635869Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8636019Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8636294Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8636577Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8638570Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8638838Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8639205Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8639510Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8639751Z     #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8640151Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8640428Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8640764Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8641221Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8641467Z     else
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8641890Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8642386Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8642538Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8642909Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643188Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643445Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643716Z       fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643950Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8644123Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8644435Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8649414Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8649771Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650071Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650269Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650456Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650584Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650812Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651023Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651294Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651486Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651669Z         fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651860Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652064Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652249Z     done
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652374Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652708Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653031Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653240Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653508Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653816Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654045Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654388Z     else
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654770Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654878Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654965Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8655579Z }

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8656508Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8659616Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680228Z # Functions to be used with livesys-* scripts
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680365Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680587Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680841Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8681575Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8681855Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8682312Z fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8682453Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8682788Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683023Z strstr() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683260Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683476Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683607Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683859Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684078Z action() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684310Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684441Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684666Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684917Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8685421Z     shift
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8685649Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8685891Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686107Z     else
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686351Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686563Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686783Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687000Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687221Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687447Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687578Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687904Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8688152Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8688548Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8688768Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689010Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689226Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689476Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689719Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690046Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690293Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690613Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690853Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691181Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691430Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691703Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691949Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692078Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692319Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692642Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692927Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693057Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693290Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693520Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693770Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693989Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8694218Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8695356Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8695622Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8695856Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696087Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696299Z         ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696526Z esac
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696653Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697061Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697283Z success() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697544Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697763Z     return 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697978Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698103Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698339Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698557Z failure() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698785Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699041Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699318Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699545Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699756Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699890Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8700111Z echo_success() {

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8700582Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701198Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701459Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701690Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701911Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8702122Z     return 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8702337Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8702464Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8704783Z }

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8705442Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8705671Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8705901Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706134Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706374Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706599Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706822Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707041Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707294Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707510Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707731Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707956Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708196Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708414Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708630Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708906Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709206Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709425Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709639Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709885Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710134Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710350Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710575Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710810Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711055Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711278Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711487Z         *)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711839Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8712078Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8712320Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8712536Z }

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8880235Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8880498Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8880775Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8881057Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8881429Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8882228Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8882393Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8882744Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883030Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883430Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883695Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883954Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8884291Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8884710Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8884952Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8885422Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8885665Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8885987Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8886136Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8886498Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8886792Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8887029Z     echo
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8887316Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8887759Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888028Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888266Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888626Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888983Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889221Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889375Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889630Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889934Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8890287Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8890651Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8890959Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891255Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891528Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891773Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891923Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8892200Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8892617Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8892871Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8893203Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8193170Z class LanguageInstallationTask(Task):

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8194188Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8195884Z     def __init__(self, sysroot, lang):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8196366Z         """Create a new task,
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8196707Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8197057Z         :param sysroot: a path to the root of the installed system
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8197680Z         :param lang: a value for LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8198186Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8198564Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8198993Z         self._sysroot = sysroot
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8199432Z         self._lang = lang
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8199756Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8200004Z     @property
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8200384Z     def name(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8200799Z         return "Configure language"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8201164Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8201410Z     def run(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8201831Z         """Run the installation task."""
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8202335Z         lang = self._get_supported_language()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8202863Z         self._write_language_configuration(lang)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8203508Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8203779Z     def _get_supported_language(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8204285Z         """Return a supported locale.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8204790Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8205272Z         :return: a locale
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8205734Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8206200Z         if self._is_language_support_installed(self._lang):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8206770Z             return self._lang

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8207470Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale is unsupported.", self._lang)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8208174Z         log.debug("Using the '%s' locale as a fallback.", self.LOCALE_FALLBACK)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8208806Z         return self.LOCALE_FALLBACK
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8209194Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8209497Z     def _is_language_support_installed(self, lang):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8210118Z         """Is the support for the specified language installed?
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8210568Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8211092Z         The language is considered to be supported if we are not
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8211782Z         able to determine the supported locales due to missing tools.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8212265Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8212587Z         :param lang: a value for the LANG locale variable
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8213283Z         :return: False if the locale is known to be not supported, otherwise True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8213924Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8214293Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8214797Z             output = execWithCapture("locale", ["-a"], root=self._sysroot)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8215518Z         except OSError as e:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8216059Z             log.warning("Couldn't get supported locales: %s", e)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8216613Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8216945Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8217297Z         match = find_best_locale_match(lang, output.splitlines())
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8217789Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8218107Z         log.debug("The '%s' locale matched '%s'.", lang, match)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8218694Z         return bool(match)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8219049Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8303628Z             fobj.write('FONT="%s"\n' % vc_font)

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8319576Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8319763Z     def _from_localed_format(layouts, variants):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8320023Z         layouts = layouts.split(",") if layouts else []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8320390Z         variants = variants.split(",") if variants else []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8320560Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8320719Z         # if there are more layouts than variants, empty strings should be appended
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8321051Z         diff = len(layouts) - len(variants)

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8324050Z     def set_layouts(self, layouts_variants, options=None, convert=False):

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8324457Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8324596Z         :param layouts_variants: list of 'layout (variant)' or 'layout'

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8325547Z         :param options: list of X11 options that should be set

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8326103Z         :param convert: whether the layouts should be converted to a VConsole keymap
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8326431Z                         (see set_and_convert_layouts)

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8326824Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8326979Z         if not self._localed_proxy:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327167Z             return
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327263Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327332Z         layouts = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327498Z         variants = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327669Z         parsing_failed = False
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327792Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8327959Z         for layout_variant in (nonempty for nonempty in layouts_variants if nonempty):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8328256Z             try:

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8329791Z             layouts.append(layout)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8329997Z             variants.append(variant)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8330131Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8330217Z         if not layouts and parsing_failed:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8330421Z             return

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8354867Z     """Localed wrapper class which is used to control compositor.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355244Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355445Z     It adds support for layout selection and reactions on the compositor system changes.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355759Z     """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355839Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8355912Z     def __init__(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356088Z         super().__init__()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356204Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356282Z         self._user_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356497Z         self._last_layouts_variants = []
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356627Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8356888Z         self.compositor_layouts_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357158Z         self.compositor_selected_layout_changed = Signal()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357333Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357487Z         # to reflect updates from the compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8357875Z         self._localed_proxy.PropertiesChanged.connect(self._on_properties_changed)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8362359Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8362536Z     def _on_properties_changed(self, interface, changed_props, invalid_props):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8363024Z         if "X11Layout" in changed_props or "X11Variant" in changed_props:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8363432Z             layouts_variants = self._from_localed_format(changed_props["X11Layout"].get_string(),
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8363813Z                                                          changed_props["X11Variant"].get_string())
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8364178Z             # This part is a bit tricky. The signal processing here means that compositor has
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8364596Z             # changed current layouts configuration. This could happen for multiple reasons:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8364950Z             # - user changed the layout in compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8365417Z             # - Anaconda set the layout to compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8365847Z             # - any other magic logic for compositor (we just don't know)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366111Z             #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366299Z             # The question is how we should behave:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366616Z             # - we don't want to take compositor layouts to Anaconda because that will change
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8366947Z             #   what user will have in the installed system.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8367272Z             # - we don't want to force our layouts to compositor because that would forbid user
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8367647Z             #   to change compositor layout when Anaconda runs in background
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8367906Z             #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368176Z             # The best shot seems to just signal out that the layout has changed and nothing else.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368438Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368568Z             # layouts has changed in compositor, always emit this signal
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8368918Z             log.debug("Localed layouts has changed. Last known: '%s' current: '%s'",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369253Z                       self._last_layouts_variants, layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369546Z             self.compositor_layouts_changed.emit(layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369727Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8369829Z             # check if last selected variant has changed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370076Z             # nothing is selected in compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370302Z             if not layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370532Z                 log.warning("Compositor layouts not set.")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8370803Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit("")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371058Z             # we don't know last used layouts
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371289Z             elif not self._last_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371694Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different. "
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8371991Z                           "Missing information about last selected layouts.")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8372325Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8372616Z             # selected (first) has changed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8372877Z             elif layouts_variants[0] != self._last_layouts_variants[0]:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373175Z                 log.debug("Compositor selected layout is different.")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373490Z                 self.compositor_selected_layout_changed.emit(layouts_variants[0])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373705Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8373808Z             self._last_layouts_variants = layouts_variants

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8400105Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8400367Z         This will search for the given layout variant in the list and move it as first
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8400995Z         in the list. The first layout in systemd is taken as the used one.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8401342Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8401604Z         :param layout_variant: The layout to set, with format "layout (variant)"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8402098Z             (e.g. "cz (qwerty)")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8402408Z         :type layout_variant: str
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8402770Z         :return: If the keyboard layout was activated
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8403144Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8403405Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8403744Z         # ignore compositor layouts but force Anaconda configuration
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404206Z         layouts = self._user_layouts_variants
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404455Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404546Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8404866Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(layouts, layout_variant)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8405430Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8405769Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8406045Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8406486Z             log.warning("Can't set layout: '%s' as first to the current set: %s",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8406983Z                         layout_variant, layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8407323Z             return False

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8407603Z     @staticmethod
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8407911Z     def _shift_list(source_layouts, value_to_first):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8408450Z         """Helper method to reorder list of layouts and move one as first in the list.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8408855Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8409130Z         We should preserve the ordering just shift items from start of the list to the
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8409649Z         end in the same order.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8409849Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410001Z         When we want to set 2nd as first in this list:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410375Z         ["cz", "es", "us"]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410659Z         The result should be:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8410947Z         ["es", "us", "cz"]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411119Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411310Z         So the compositor has the same next layout as Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411624Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8411867Z         :raises: ValueError: if the list is small or the layout is not inside
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8412326Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8412620Z         value_id = source_layouts.index(value_to_first)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8413194Z         new_list = source_layouts[value_id:len(source_layouts)] + source_layouts[0:value_id]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8413882Z         return new_list
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414054Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414173Z     def select_next_layout(self):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414555Z         """Select (make it first) next layout for compositor.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8414851Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8416208Z         Find current compositor layout in the list of defined layouts and set next to it as
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8416965Z         current (first) for compositor. We need to have user defined list because compositor
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8417752Z         layouts will change with the selection. Store this list when user is setting configuration
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8418417Z         to compositor. This list must not change ordering.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8418716Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8418946Z         :param user_layouts: List of layouts selected by user in Anaconda.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8419443Z         :type user_layouts: [str]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8419999Z         :return: If switch was successful True otherwise False
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8420418Z         :rtype: bool
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8420687Z         """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8420966Z         current_layout = self.current_layout_variant
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8421330Z         layout_id = 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8421498Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8421636Z         if not self._user_layouts_variants:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8422178Z             log.error("Can't switch next layout - user defined keyboard layout is not present!")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8422739Z             return False
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8422910Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8423021Z         # find next layout
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8423384Z         for i, v in enumerate(self._user_layouts_variants):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8423777Z             if v == current_layout:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424099Z                 layout_id = i + 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424478Z                 layout_id %= len(self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424757Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8424862Z         try:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8425545Z             new_layouts = self._shift_list(self._user_layouts_variants,
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8426051Z                                            self._user_layouts_variants[layout_id])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8426486Z             self._set_layouts(new_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8426840Z             return True
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8427111Z         except ValueError:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8427585Z             log.warning("Can't set next keyboard layout %s", self._user_layouts_variants)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8428081Z             return False

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528226Z from pyanaconda.core.constants import DEFAULT_KEYBOARD

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528515Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528518Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528688Z def get_missing_keyboard_configuration(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528792Z     """Get keyboard configuration if not set by user.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528795Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528868Z     Algorithm works as this:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8528982Z     1. Check if full keyboard configuration is already set
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529056Z        -> return these
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529183Z     2. If no X layouts, check if they can be obtained from live system
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529256Z        -> read X layouts
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529344Z     3a. If still no configuration is present
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529483Z        -> use DEFAULT_KEYBOARD as console layout and fall through to...
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529624Z     3b. If only one of the keyboard layout or virtual console keymap is set
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529713Z        -> convert one to the other by localed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529820Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8529968Z     :param localed_wrapper: instance of systemd-localed service wrapper
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530052Z     :type localed_wrapper: LocaledWrapper
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530159Z     :param x_layouts: list of X layout specifications
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530233Z     :type x_layouts: list(str)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530349Z     :param vc_keymap: virtual console keyboard mapping name
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530423Z     :type vc_keymap: str
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530526Z     :returns: tuple of X layouts and VC keyboard settings
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530604Z     :rtype: (list(str), str))
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530669Z     """
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530744Z     if vc_keymap and x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8530936Z         log.debug("Keyboard layouts and virtual console keymap already set - nothing to do")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531015Z         return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531096Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531198Z     live_keyboard = get_live_keyboard_instance()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531382Z     # layouts are not set by user, we should take a look for live configuration if available
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531458Z     if not x_layouts and live_keyboard:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531598Z         log.debug("Keyboard configuration from Live system is available")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531698Z         x_layouts = live_keyboard.read_keyboard_layouts()
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531701Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531779Z     if not vc_keymap and not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8531969Z         log.debug("Using default value %s for missing virtual console keymap", DEFAULT_KEYBOARD)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532042Z         vc_keymap = DEFAULT_KEYBOARD
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532045Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532124Z     if not vc_keymap or not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532327Z         x_layouts, vc_keymap = _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532333Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532401Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532407Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532409Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532564Z def _resolve_missing_by_conversion(localed_wrapper, x_layouts, vc_keymap):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532632Z     if not vc_keymap:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532748Z         vc_keymap = localed_wrapper.convert_layouts(x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532921Z         log.debug("Missing virtual console keymap value %s converted from %s X layouts",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8532993Z                   vc_keymap, x_layouts)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533062Z     if not x_layouts:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533171Z         x_layouts = localed_wrapper.convert_keymap(vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533334Z         log.debug("Missing X layouts value %s converted from %s virtual console keymap",
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533408Z                   x_layouts, vc_keymap)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533411Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8533481Z     return x_layouts, vc_keymap

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8618537Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8618781Z # Run a local bridge, web server, and browser for a particular Cockpit page.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619042Z # This is useful for integration into .desktop files, for systems which don't
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619299Z # have cockpit.socket enabled. The web server and browser run in an unshared
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619538Z # network namespace, and thus are totally isolated from everything else.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619633Z #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619725Z # Usage:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8619987Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620230Z # For testing purposes one can also make cockpit-ws bind to all addresses
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620429Z # (this is insecure and should not be used in prodcuction):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620675Z #     cockpit-desktop /cockpit/@localhost/system/index.html -t default -r 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620761Z #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620861Z set -eu
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620866Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8620971Z THEME_ID="default"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621064Z WEBUI_REMOTE=0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621177Z while getopts t:r: option
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621262Z do
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621363Z     case "${option}"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621458Z         in
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621564Z         t)THEME_ID=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621681Z         r)WEBUI_REMOTE=${OPTARG};;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621845Z         *) echo "Usage: $0 [-t THEME_ID] [-r WEBUI_REMOTE]" >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8621941Z        exit 1 ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8622107Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8622190Z done
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8628130Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8635849Z URL_PATH="/cockpit/@localhost/anaconda-webui/index.html"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8636015Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8636291Z WEBUI_ADDRESS="127.0.0.1"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8636574Z if [[ "$WEBUI_REMOTE" == "1" ]]

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8638567Z # Function to setup user-related environment
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8638836Z setup_user_environment() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8639202Z     # PKEXEC_UID is the uid for "gnome-initial-setup" or "liveuser"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8639507Z     # depending on how the installer gets started.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8639748Z     #
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8640149Z     # It's unset on non-live-images, so we just use the current user then (root)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8640422Z     if [ -n "${PKEXEC_UID:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8640762Z       # If PKEXEC_UID is set, use the user associated with it
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8641218Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(id -n -u "${PKEXEC_UID}")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8641464Z     else
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8641880Z       # Try to find another user who is not root and has /usr/bin/bash as their shell
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8642384Z       INSTALLER_USER=$(grep '/usr/bin/bash' /etc/passwd | grep -v '^root' | cut -d: -f1 | head -n 1)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8642535Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8642906Z       # If no such user is found, use the current user running the script
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643185Z       if [ -z "${INSTALLER_USER:-}" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643443Z         INSTALLER_USER=$(id -un)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643713Z       fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8643948Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8644121Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8644433Z     # Get the home directory of the selected user

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8649412Z get_default_browser() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8649768Z     # Read the default browser from the anaconda runtime configuration file but wait till it's available
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650064Z     # FIXME: Related to: https://issues.redhat.com/browse/INSTALLER-4223
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650262Z     timeout=60
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650454Z     elapsed=0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650583Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8650810Z     while [ ! -f /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf ]; do
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651021Z         if [ "$elapsed" -ge "$timeout" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651291Z             echo "Timed out waiting for anaconda.conf to be available." >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651484Z             exit 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651667Z         fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8651858Z         sleep 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652062Z         elapsed=$((elapsed + 1))
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652247Z     done
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652372Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8652705Z     # FIXME: Should be provided as an argument to this script instead of grepping the config file
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653029Z     DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(grep '^webui_web_engine =' /run/anaconda/anaconda.conf | cut -d'=' -f2)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653235Z     if [ -n "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653506Z         echo "Default browser set in anaconda.conf: $DEFAULT_BROWSER" >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8653814Z         DEFAULT_BROWSER=$(echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER" | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//;s/[[:space:]]*$//')
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654044Z         echo "$DEFAULT_BROWSER"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654385Z     else
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654763Z         echo "No default browser set in anaconda.conf, using firefox." >&2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654875Z         echo "firefox"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8654963Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8655575Z }

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8656491Z     FIREFOX_PROFILE_PATH="${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR}/anaconda/firefox-profile"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8656505Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8659199Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8659615Z

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8679940Z #!/bin/sh
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680224Z # Functions to be used with livesys-* scripts
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680361Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680584Z # Check if gettext exists
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8680839Z if ! type gettext > /dev/null 2>&1; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8681571Z   # If not, create a dummy function that returns the input verbatim
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8681849Z   gettext() { printf '%s' "$1"; }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8682136Z fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8682450Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8682785Z # returns OK if $1 contains literal string $2 (and isn't empty)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683020Z strstr() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683256Z     [ "${1##*"$2"*}" != "$1" ]
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683473Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683604Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8683857Z # Run some action. Log its output.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684075Z action() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684308Z     local STRING rc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684439Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684663Z     STRING=$1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8684913Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8685417Z     shift
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8685647Z     if "$@"; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8685889Z         success "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686104Z     else
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686348Z         failure "$(gettext "${STRING} ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686560Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686781Z     rc=$?
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8686998Z     printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687219Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687438Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687576Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8687901Z # verbose ->> very (very!) old bootup look (prior to RHL-6.0?)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8688149Z # color ->> default bootup look
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8688544Z # other ->> default bootup look without ANSI colors or positioning
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8688766Z BOOTUP=color
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689007Z # Column to start "[  OK  ]" label in:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689224Z RES_COL=60
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689473Z # terminal sequence to move to that column:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8689716Z MOVE_TO_COL="printf \\033[${RES_COL}G"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690043Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'success' (bright green):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690290Z SETCOLOR_SUCCESS="printf \\033[1;32m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690610Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'failure' (bright red):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8690851Z SETCOLOR_FAILURE="printf \\033[1;31m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691178Z # Terminal sequence to set color to a 'warning' (bright yellow):
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691423Z SETCOLOR_WARNING="printf \\033[1;33m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691700Z # Terminal sequence to reset to the default color:
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8691946Z SETCOLOR_NORMAL="printf \\033[0;39m"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692076Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692317Z # NOTE: /dev/ttyS* is serial console.
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692640Z # 'not a tty' is reported when executed under systemd service units
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8692924Z # where /dev/null is associated with standard input
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693054Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693288Z test_tty=$(LC_ALL=C tty)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693517Z case "$test_tty" in
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693768Z     /dev/ttyS* | 'not a tty')
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8693986Z         BOOTUP=serial
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8694215Z         MOVE_TO_COL=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8695349Z         SETCOLOR_SUCCESS=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8695619Z         SETCOLOR_FAILURE=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8695853Z         SETCOLOR_WARNING=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696085Z         SETCOLOR_NORMAL=
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696297Z         ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696516Z esac
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8696650Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697058Z # Log that something succeeded
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697281Z success() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697541Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_success
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697761Z     return 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8697976Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698101Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698337Z # Log that something failed
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698555Z failure() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8698783Z     local rc=$?
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699039Z     [ "$BOOTUP" != "verbose" ] && echo_failure
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699316Z     [ -x /bin/plymouth ] && /bin/plymouth --details
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699543Z     return $rc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699753Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8699888Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8700108Z echo_success() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8700361Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $MOVE_TO_COL
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8700580Z     printf '%s' "["
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8700837Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_SUCCESS
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701085Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "  OK  ")"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701457Z     [ "$BOOTUP" = "color" ] && $SETCOLOR_NORMAL
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701687Z     printf '%s' "]"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8701909Z     printf '\r'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8702120Z     return 0
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8702335Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8702462Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8702681Z echo_failure() {

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8703149Z     printf '%s' "["

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8703646Z     printf '%s' "$(gettext "FAILED")"

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8704568Z     return 1
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8704781Z }

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8705439Z fscheck() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8705668Z     local fstype="$1"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8705892Z     local fs="$2"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706132Z     local d="${3:-$2}"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706371Z     umount "$fs" >/dev/null 2>&1 || :
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706597Z     case "$fstype" in
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8706820Z         ext[432])
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707038Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707292Z             flock "$d" e2fsck -yfv "$fs" || :
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707507Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707728Z         vfat|msdos)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8707954Z             printf '\n'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708193Z             flock "$d" fsck.fat -avVw "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708411Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708628Z         btrfs)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8708903Z             printf '# btrfs check -p --repair --force'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709203Z             flock "$d" btrfs check -p --repair --force "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709419Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709637Z         xfs)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8709883Z             printf '\n# xfs_repair -v'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710127Z             flock "$d" xfs_repair -v "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710347Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710573Z         f2fs)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8710808Z             printf '# fsck.f2fs -afy'
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711052Z             flock "$d" fsck.f2fs -afy "$fs"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711271Z             ;;
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711485Z         *)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8711836Z             printf "\n\tERROR:   Filesystem checking for '%s' is not\n \
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8712076Z                 available.\n" "$fstype"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8712318Z     esac
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8712533Z }

2026-10-25T08:17:53.8880232Z mountPersistentHome() {
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8880495Z   # support label/uuid
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8880772Z   if [ "${homedev##LABEL=}" != "$homedev" ] ||
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8881055Z     [ "${homedev##UUID=}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8881426Z     homedev=$(blkid -o device -t "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8882224Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8882390Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8882741Z   # if we're given a file rather than a blockdev, loop mount it
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883021Z   if [ "${homedev##mtd}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883427Z     # mtd devs don't have a block device but get magic-mounted with -t jffs2
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883692Z     mountopts="-t jffs2"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8883952Z   elif [ ! -b "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8884288Z     if [ "${homedev##/run/initramfs/live}" != "$homedev" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8884708Z       action "Remounting live store r/w" mount -o remount,rw /run/initramfs/live
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8884950Z     fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8885418Z     homedev=$(losetup -f --show "$homedev")
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8885662Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8885984Z   homefs=$(blkid -s TYPE -o value "$homedev" 2>/dev/null)
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8886134Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8886496Z   # if it's encrypted, we need to unlock it
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8886789Z   if [ "$homefs" = "crypto_LUKS" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8887027Z     echo
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8887313Z     echo "Setting up encrypted /home device"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8887751Z     plymouth ask-for-password --command="cryptsetup luksOpen $homedev EncHome"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888026Z     homedev=/dev/mapper/EncHome
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888264Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888618Z   if [ "$homedev" ] && [ "${cmdline##* rd.skipfsck[= ]}" = "$cmdline" ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8888980Z     action "Checking home filesystem" fscheck "$homefs" "$homedev"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889219Z   fi
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889373Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889628Z   # and finally do the mount
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8889931Z   mount ${mountopts:+$mountopts} "$homedev" /home
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8890285Z   # if we have /home under what's passed for persistent home, then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8890649Z   # we should make that the real /home.  useful for mtd device on olpc
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8890956Z   [ -d /home/home ] && mount --bind /home/home /home
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891252Z   [ -x /sbin/restorecon ] && /sbin/restorecon /home
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891526Z   [ -d /home/liveuser ] && USERADDARGS="-M"
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891767Z }
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8891920Z
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8892197Z cut=${cmdline##* persistenthome[= ]}
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8892615Z if [ ${#cut} -ne ${#cmdline} ]; then
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8892868Z   homedev=${cut%% *}
2026-10-25T08:17:53.8893201Z elif [ -e /run/initramfs/live/"${livedir}"/home.img ]; then
